##### Focused artifact namespace and immutable attempt identity

Focused evidence must live under the sibling existing-artifacts namespace
`.devai/state/check-cache/v1/artifacts/mutation-focused/`, never beneath
`.devai/state/check-cache/v1/artifacts/mutation/`. The full-roster publisher must remain
byte-identical and must be mechanically unable to rename, replace, traverse, or clean the focused
root.

Each focused attempt is keyed by the exact package stem, 40-hex candidate commit, and governed
64-hex diff digest. Within that immutable attempt identity, success and failure have distinct final
paths. Publication is create-only: no existing success, failure, candidate, package, or focused
root may be renamed, replaced, truncated, or removed. A collision stops before mutation when
detected at preflight and fails publication if detected later. A failure can never replace a
success, and neither kind can replace an earlier artifact. Because D24.12 authorizes only one later
attempt, an unexpected pre-existing final path is a hard stop, not a reason to invent a nonce or
delete evidence.

The staging directory must be a unique sibling beneath the focused root, fixed to the same attempt
identity and process, created with mode `0700`. The publisher writes only into that new staging
directory, forces every evidence file to mode `0600`, fsyncs or otherwise closes every complete
file before publication, revalidates candidate and artifact bounds, and performs one rename from
the complete staging directory to the previously absent final success or failure path. Failure
before that rename removes only the current uncommitted staging directory. Failure after a
successful rename must not delete or roll back the newly published immutable evidence. No backup
path is permitted because no published path is replaceable.

The canonical persisted success files are exactly one normalized report, one result, and one
manifest. The report is at most 67,108,864 bytes, the result at most 65,536 bytes, and the manifest
at most 65,536 bytes, each including its terminal LF; their combined maximum is 67,239,936 bytes.
The report ceiling equals the runner's existing 64-MiB subprocess-output bound and is four times
the existing 16-MiB single-text bound. Before patch approval, the exact-ten-target, 808-identity
synthetic current-shape report must measure at most 50,331,648 persisted bytes, proving at least 25%
headroom without using live mutation output. A failure attempt contains exactly one manifest of at
most 65,536 bytes. Bounds are checked against the exact bytes to be written before any file is
created and again before publication. Oversize, missing, extra, non-regular, symlinked, or
wrong-mode content stops and publishes nothing.

Every digest in result or manifest is SHA-256 over the exact persisted bytes including the terminal
LF. The manifest binds its own schema and kind; package and workspace; candidate commit and tree;
the governed diff digest; clean-index status; exact allowed unstaged paths and modes; package,
configuration, source-set, target-set, report, and result digests; process error-absence, status,
and signal; duration; thresholds; score; complete eight-status totals; exact target-file count;
total, scored, and non-scored identity counts; and the final evidence-relative paths. No digest may
stand in for a missing required count or identity field.

##### Exact candidate and drift binding

The governed diff digest is the exact byte output of the following fixed configuration and argv:

```text
git -c core.abbrev=9 -c color.ui=false -c diff.noprefix=false -c diff.mnemonicprefix=false -c diff.algorithm=myers -c diff.indentHeuristic=true -c core.autocrlf=false diff --no-ext-diff --no-textconv --binary HEAD --
```

On the frozen four-spec candidate it must equal
`fa412ac92080b5ebd183c2cb05034a16ecce68880dee8b7770a5dedf216ba6f9`.
The fixed nine-hex abbreviation is part of the governed bytes and prevents repository growth or
`core.abbrev` configuration from changing the digest. `--full-index`, text reconstruction, patch
parsing, alternate base, staged changes, and composite digests are forbidden. The sensor must also
prove that changing abbreviation width or any pinned diff option changes the bytes and fails the
expected digest. The index must be clean. Porcelain status must contain exactly the four authorized
unstaged mode-`0644` worklist specs and no other tracked or untracked path. Declared package-local
raw report, build, incremental, and mutation-temporary paths are checked separately and may exist
only during their authorized lifecycle; every other ignored-output change stops.

Before build, before Stryker, immediately after normalized-report parsing, immediately before
publication, and immediately after publication, the runner revalidates HEAD, tree, clean index,
exact porcelain allowlist, four file modes and complete file digests, exact governed diff bytes and
digest, package/config/source/target inputs, and absence of unexpected tracked, untracked, or
ignored drift. The post-publication check treats only the exact newly published focused path as an
expected ignored addition. Any disagreement stops; published immutable failure evidence survives.

The status-zero diagnostic rule remains as D24.11 specified: only a fully normalized report plus
absent spawn error, null signal, and status zero can disregard workstation-path text found solely
in non-retained stdout or stderr. Credential-shaped material in any diagnostic remains fatal.
Every retained key and value remains credential-scanned, portable-path strict, and bounded. Spawn
error, signal, nonzero status, missing or unsafe report, below-break score, and all existing
classification precedence remain fail closed.

##### Missing per-worktree post-merge receipt adapter

The combined D24.11 fast-forward returned zero and reached the exact expected HEAD and tree, but its

mode-`0700` issuer, SHA-256
`7d9a43780d1f78d023dc95181f750251a022d3a0dff914a0f25fd3225b0280c5`, and a mode-`0600` key; the
recovered Inspector worktree Git directory lacks both. Git does not roll back a completed merge
because a post-merge hook fails, so the exact commit/tree integration remains valid, but the absent
receipt is a host-adapter readiness blocker and cannot be treated as success.

Only DEVAI Architect host-adapter provisioning is authorized for this exact recovered worktree.
It may create only its per-worktree Git-dir `devai/` directory, mode `0700`, and copy from the
resolved common Git-dir exactly the existing issuer and secret key: issuer mode `0700` with the
digest above, key mode `0600`, verified byte-identical with a non-output comparison. It must not
copy the common receipt, print or hash the key, edit tracked hooks, change `core.hooksPath`, alter
Git config, touch another worktree Git dir, rotate credentials, or remove any common or worktree
adapter file.

tracked `.devai/config/post-merge-host-adapter.json` exists and matches the issuer's declared

executable from the frozen dependency graph at the required DEVAI version. Absence, wrong version,

After provisioning and those exact HEAD/tree/status/executable/attestation checks, Architect may

mode-`0600` receipt in that worktree Git dir, correct exact merge SHA and tracked attestation

no full-roster summary, and left no staging or package-local raw mutation report. There was no retry.

This is not idempotency mutation-quality evidence. A subsequent ordinary idempotency unit run
passed all 65 tests, while the ordinary integration tier exited 1 in approximately 2.5 seconds.
Every `STYNX_TEST_PG_*` control and database URL was absent, the selected Docker daemon was
unreachable, and no PostgreSQL endpoint responded. Other installed Docker contexts did not contain
a running dependency. Idempotency's unchanged Stryker Vitest population includes both unit and
integration specs; its integration setup requires Redis through the selected Docker daemon and a
PostgreSQL database. The full roster therefore began even though a known later member could not
execute in the inherited environment. Because every complete roster necessarily includes that
member, Docker and PostgreSQL readiness are whole-roster preconditions, not permission to skip or
degrade one package.

The outer path reason does not prove that the report normalized. The runner's unsafe-report catch
and an above-floor normalized report with a nonzero child can both surface the same bounded reason.
The deleted stdout, stderr, and report bytes cannot distinguish those paths and remain prohibited
from reconstruction or retention. Idempotency item 23 is **unobserved**: it neither passed nor
produced a mutation score, census, survivor, or product-defect claim. Classifier normalization
provenance remains open, and mid-roster infrastructure loss remains a known residual. D24.15 does
not authorize a classifier, sanitizer, idempotency, target, threshold, or test-population change.

The existing repository contracts are the sole preflight source. `scripts/devai-local-rc.mjs`
requires exact PostgreSQL `HOST`, `PORT`, `USER`, and `TEMPLATE` controls and checks them with
`pg_isready`; `scripts/ci-local/run.sh` fails when the currently selected Docker daemon is
unreachable. Engineer may reuse only those checks in `scripts/lib/mutation-evidence.mjs` and call
them from `scripts/run-mutation-evidence.mjs`. The no-package full-roster path must execute the

The preflight is read-only and deterministic. It first validates the four exact PostgreSQL control
names with no aliases or defaults, then checks the currently selected Docker daemon with the
existing `docker info` predicate, then invokes `pg_isready` with the exact accepted controls. It

change environment values, or access a credential outside the existing mutation allowlist. A
missing PostgreSQL control, unreachable Docker daemon, or unsuccessful PostgreSQL readiness check

Failure output is one fixed bounded JSON object and no artifact publication. Its exhaustive fields

`classification: "mutation-harness-failure"`, one allowlisted `reason`,
`packagesStarted: 0`, and a `preflight` object containing only fixed Docker and PostgreSQL states.
Docker is `ready` or `unreachable`; PostgreSQL is `missing-controls`, `ready`, `unreachable`, or
`not-checked`. Reason precedence is exact and first-match: `missing-postgres-controls`, then
`docker-unreachable`, then `postgres-unreachable`. The simultaneous missing-controls,
Docker-unreachable, and PostgreSQL-unreachable fixture must therefore retain the first reason,

#### D24.22 — Filesystem-URL portability correction before gate restart

The D24.21 gate authorization was consumed at gate 1 on exact tracked-clean candidate
`e3b4844b62014a619b9bd4e416ca93d1fbf748dc`, tree
`abaae9024942a7bd5caa342a27c7e6ded6c26e24`. The one direct
`TURBO_FORCE=true pnpm ci:stynx` process returned status 1, null signal, and no signal after
approximately 11 seconds. Its fixed terminal classification is `check:engines` root-manifest path
identity failure: the selected physical worktree path contains a space, the script retained the
percent-encoded filesystem URL pathname, and the root `package.json` read returned `ENOENT`. No
later `ci:stynx` subcommand began. Gates 2 through 8 and PREPARE remain at zero. There was no retry,
alternate command, cleanup, or state repair, and the candidate remained exact and clean.

This is a filesystem-URL portability defect, not an engine-policy, package-manifest, dependency,
environment, or infrastructure failure. `scripts/check-engines.mjs` line 5 obtains the repository
root through `new URL('..', import.meta.url).pathname`. URL pathname text is not an operating-system
filesystem path: percent encoding remains significant to `fs` and caused the observed lookup to
address a nonexistent identity. The exact correction is Node's `fileURLToPath`, applied once to the
`file:` URL. `decodeURI`, `decodeURIComponent`, literal `%20` replacement, or any other compensating
decoder is forbidden because it can double-decode literal percent content and weakens the platform
contract.

Read-only census found the same closure-reachable defect in all three `cwd` values at lines 50, 58,
and 66 of `reference/web/playwright.config.mjs`. Fixing only gate 1 would knowingly leave the
D24.17 gate-2 root E2E configuration unable to represent the same space-containing filesystem path.
D24.22 therefore includes those three uses in the same narrowly classified defect. It does not
infer that gate 2 has run or failed; the configuration regression tests below are preflight only,
and the complete root E2E process remains mandatory after a corrected gate 1 passes.

The complete governed path population for D24.22 is exactly five paths across separate role
commits:

- Architect now changes only this campaign ADR.
- Inspector may later change only `test/scripts/local-rc-blocker-contract.test.mjs`.
- Engineer may later change only `scripts/check-engines.mjs` and
  `reference/web/playwright.config.mjs`.
- After both downstream commits pass independent review, Architect may change only `law/trace.json`
  to rebind the actual assertion count and assertion digest of the existing local-RC contract test,
  together with a final ADR checkpoint only if required to authorize the fresh gate sequence.

No new test path is authorized, so the executable-test path census remains unchanged. Package
manifests, engine values, dependency or lock files, Playwright tests, E2E commands, task graphs,
timeouts, thresholds, ignore files, external controls, retained evidence, and every other source,
test, configuration, trace, or policy path remain outside the Inspector and Engineer checkpoints.
Each role uses its own declared session and commit; no discipline sets and actuates against its own
reference.

The binding invariants are:

- every filesystem `file:` URL in the two authorized plant files is converted exactly once through
  `fileURLToPath`, never through `.pathname` or a compensating text decoder;
- the engine rules, supported versions, package discovery population, failure aggregation, and
  successful output of `check-engines` remain byte-semantically unchanged;
- every Playwright web-server `cwd` is the decoded absolute `reference/web` directory in both OIDC
  modes, while every command, URL, port, wait expression, timeout, and `reuseExistingServer` value
  remains unchanged;
- no source, package, configuration, test, assertion, threshold, mutation target, or security rule
  is weakened; and
- every child-process acceptance requires error absence, status exactly zero, and null signal.

Inspector first creates RED evidence solely in the existing local-RC contract test. For
`check-engines`, the test copies the exact script bytes into a temporary root whose basename
contains a literal space, supplies only a minimal valid root manifest, and first proves that the
copied program has no relative or package import beyond its current Node built-ins. It then spawns
those exact bytes and requires error absence, status exactly zero, and null signal; status `null`,

For Playwright, the same Inspector test copies the exact configuration bytes into a separate

`1`. The prior environment is restored in `finally`. The two independent evaluations must have

in both authorized plant files. Each fixture is confined and removed within its test; residue or

Only after the Inspector RED checkpoint may Engineer replace the root calculation in
`scripts/check-engines.mjs` with `fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url))`, importing
`fileURLToPath` from `node:url`. Engineer also converts the three Playwright `cwd` values through

The bounded correction floor consists of the frozen named D24.22 test pattern in the existing

change is strictly strengthening: a call matching `mapEventRow(eventRow)` proves the prior bare
called condition, while the converse need not hold. `mapEventRow` and `eventRow` are existing
